### Decisions and attribution

At 286.4s, squad 3 chose FightHere: nearest known group ([trace 496538](#trace-496538)), followed by 7 shots and 2 own casualties; estimate 25.4 against 10 distinct squad-reported contacts; At 172.5s, squad 2 chose answered a neighbour with support by fire ([trace 253777](#trace-253777)), followed by 31 shots and 1 own casualties; estimate 23.4 against 0 distinct squad-reported contacts; At 162.0s, squad 6 chose renewed the existing objective ([trace 234194](#trace-234194)), followed by 28 shots and 1 own casualties; estimate 19.3 against 7 distinct squad-reported contacts. These are observed sequences, not proof of causation.

### Communication
